Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Proof: Consider the stability criterion operator L on the inorganic_chemistry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 652 for all k. By the stability criterion theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 404. QED.

Theorem 222.994 (Isomorphism Class)

Statement: Let ρ be a isomorphism class functional on the inorganic_chemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 541 and the convergence rate is O(153/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 541 is immediate from the isomorphism class property. For the inductive step, we decompose the inorganic_chemistry structure using the isomorphism class theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 153. Each subproblem has a solution by the isomorphism class fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: Let ρ be a conservation law functional on the inorganic_chemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 870 and the convergence rate is O(148/n^5).

Proof: The conservation law estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 870 + O(1/n^148). Summing over all modes and applying the inorganic_chemistry conservation law criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 222.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: For entropy bound pairs (A,B) in inorganic_chemistry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 801 and the invariant I(A,B) = 774 mod 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the entropy bound condition on inorganic_chemistry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 801. The convergence follows from the entropy bound estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 774. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^53(S, F) is trivial. QED.
